# Skills · P · part 207

823 entries, proof-carrying-agent-messages to propale-doc-reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **proof-carrying-agent-messages**: Construct and verify Proof-Carrying Messages (PCM) for multi-agent communication across A2A, MCP, and HTTP transports. Replaces blind trust in sender-signed claims with offline verification of Merkle-proven execution records from an attesting signer, three-valued claim semantics (proven / unproven / failed), T1-vs-T2 attestation tiers, person-rooted attenuating grant chains, restart-safe bounded replay protection, and parser-differential-free strict readings. Derived from Ghosh (arXiv:2610.39109). ([Marontis/agentic-engineering](https://github.com/Marontis/agentic-engineering/tree/HEAD/skills/proof-carrying-agent-messages) · ★3)
- **proof-carrying-code-generator**: Generate executable code together with formal proofs certifying safety and correctness properties in Isabelle/HOL or Coq. Use when building verified software, safety-critical systems, or when formal guarantees are required. Produces code with accompanying proofs for memory safety, bounds checking, functional correctness, invariant preservation, and termination. Supports extraction to OCaml/Haskell/SML and integration with existing codebases. ([ArabelaTso/Skills-4-SE](https://github.com/ArabelaTso/Skills-4-SE/tree/HEAD/skills/proof-carrying-code-generator) · ★260)
- **proof-carrying-release**: Audit, execute, resume, or close a production software release whose exact source revision must pass Graphite-stack CI, isolated candidate or canary, semantic, trace, and canonical user-surface gates before promotion, with rollback receipts and recoverable only-main cleanup. Use for ship/deploy/release requests, failed-rollout recovery, exact live proof, or "merge the stack and leave only main." Do not use for feature implementation, local-only QA, or ordinary CI. ([HomenShum/proof-carrying-release](https://github.com/HomenShum/proof-carrying-release/tree/HEAD/skills/proof-carrying-release))

- **propale-doc-reviewer**: Revue critique indépendante d'une trame de proposition commerciale selon trois axes simultanés. Lance 3 sous-agents spécialisés en parallèle (storytelling, cohérence, pertinence) — chacun écrit son analyse dans un fichier temporaire. Un 4ème agent de synthèse lit ces fichiers et produit le rapport final. L'orchestrateur ne touche jamais au contenu des fichiers.
